EngBench

Engineering design tasks graded by deterministic solvers, never by an LLM. Single-shot: one answer, no tools, the engineer answering cold. Agentic: a Python tool to compute and self-check, and a test lab: each submitted design comes back with a test and review report of what failed (a fuse ruptured in the short-circuit test, the plant could not charge in winter), never the cause or the numbers, and the design passes if it clears the tests within three rounds. It measures whether the model can find the root cause of its own mistakes and fix them without breaking something else. The first-design rate is shown beside it. Feedback is the superseded early loop (grader with full numbers, no compute), kept for the record. Cell footnotes are the number of task runs behind each rate.

126 tasks · 1020 task runs · 6 models · updated 2026-10-25 11:12 UTC

Leaderboard

Score out of 100: six levels, each worth published points (15, 20, 15, 20, 15, 15), earned at that level's pass rate; agentic counts a pass within three test rounds. Levels a model has not run earn nothing, so compare scores only across the same levels run.

By family

The first three families (mechanical fit, DC circuits, and their combination) are saturated and stay as a floor. The generated families ask for a design within 5% of the brute-force optimum, not just a feasible one.

   "requirement": "Overload protection of the string DC cable (I_n <= I_z and I_2 <= 1.45 I_z, clause 433.1). The 315 A string fuse is not coordinated for overload with the C-50 cable, whose derated capacity is about 147 A.",
   "reason": "The string cable cannot carry overload current. Each string feeds only its own PCS DC input. The PCS current control and the BMS limit string current to the operating maximum, about 113 A at end-of-life minimum voltage and full power. That is below the derated cable capacity of 234 A x 0.87 x 0.72, about 147 A. The cable remains protected against short circuit to clause 434 by the gBat fuse. The fuse is rated 1500 V DC and 3 ms (above the 2 ms L/R) with 100 kA breaking capacity, against about 71 kA prospective. Its let-through I2t is far below k2S2 = 143^2 x 50^2."
  }

   "requirement": "Overload protection of the string DC cable by the string fuse (I_n <= I_z and I_2 <= 1.45 I_z). The gBat 400 A string fuse (the only 1500 V fuse rating with a time constant of at least the 5 ms circuit L/R) exceeds the derated C-95 ampacity of about 237 A (362 A x 0.91 at 40 C x 0.72 for 6 circuits per tray).",
   "reason": "Under 433.3.1 b), overload protection may be omitted where a conductor is not likely to carry overload current, has no branch circuits, and is protected against short circuit. All three conditions hold. String current is limited to about 55 A at rated power and end of life, and the PCS DC limit is 1258 A / 6 inputs = 210 A per input, both below I_z; the BMS and PCS current limits plus the string contactor enforce this. Each cable is a single point-to-point string-to-PCS circuit with no branches. The 1500 V, 10 ms, 200 kA gBat fuse clears every short-circuit current, from about 3.3 kA (end of life, 5% SOC) to about 35 kA (bus-fed), within the cable k2S2 withstand (143^2 x 95^2 = 1.8e8 A2s)."
  }
 ]
}

bess3_016Grid-connected LFP battery storage system–––
S1
{
 "cells_series": 400,
 "strings_parallel": 18,
 "pcs": "PCS-1",
 "pcs_count": 4,
 "transformer": "TX-5",
 "string_fuse": "FU-13",
 "string_contactor": "CT-2",
 "dc_cable": "C-50",
 "dc_cables_parallel": 1,
 "cooling_unit": "CU-1",
 "cooling_count": 5,
 "mv_switchgear": "RMU-4",
 "deviations": [
  {
   "standard": "IEC 60364-4-43",
   "clause": "433.3.1 (b)",
   "requirement": "Overload protection of the string DC cable by coordinating the device with the cable (I_n <= I_z and I_2 <= 1.45 I_z). The cable is C-50 with I_z of about 138 A after the 50 C ambient factor (0.82) and the 6-circuit grouping factor (0.72). The string fuse is a 250 A gBat.",
   "reason": "The string cable cannot carry overload current. Its current is set by the PCS current limit and the BMS, which opens the string contactor (CT-2, breaking capacity 2000 A) on overcurrent. The worst-case string current is about 134 A at 2.75 MW, which is the higher of 1.25 x 2 MW (FCR) and the 2.2 MW margin requirement, taken with a 10% margin, at end of life and 10% SOC. That is below I_z of 138 A. The cable has no branches or socket outlets. It is protected against short circuit by the 1500 V, 3 ms, 200 kA gBat fuse FU-13: the prospective fault current is about 33 kA (own string about 7.8 kA, plus backfeed from the other strings, plus the PCS contribution of 1.64 kA), the circuit L/R of 2 ms is within the fuse's rated 3 ms, and the fuse let-through I2t is far below k2S2 of about 5.1e7 A2s."
  }
 ]
}

fuse rating 250 A exceeds the corrected cable ampacity I_z 138.2 A (1 x 234 A x k_T x k_G for 6 circuits); recorded deviation not accepted: the corrected cable ampacity 138.2 A is below the 153 A the string can carry (cell continuous limit), so the cable can be overloaded
